(interestRate / 100) / 12 : 0;\n  const semiAnnualRate = interestRate > 0 ? (interestRate / 100) / 2 : 0;\n\n  // ボーナス返済割合の正規化 (0〜40% を 0〜0.4 に変換)\n  const bonusRatio = Math.min(Math.max(bonusPaymentRatio, 0), 40) / 100;\n  const bonusPrincipal = Math.round(loanAmount * bonusRatio);\n  const monthlyPrincipal = loanAmount - bonusPrincipal;\n\n  // 1. 月々返済額とボーナス返済額の計算\n  let baseMonthlyPayment = 0;\n  let baseBonusPayment = 0;\n  let firstMonthPayment = 0;\n  let lastMonthPayment = 0;\n\n  if (repaymentType === 'equal-principal-and-interest') {\n    // 元利均等返済\n    if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n      baseMonthlyPayment = monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths;\n      baseBonusPayment = totalBonusPayments > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments : 0;\n    } else {\n      const compoundFactorMonthly = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, totalMonths);\n      baseMonthlyPayment = (monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate * compoundFactorMonthly) / (compoundFactorMonthly - 1);\n\n      if (bonusPrincipal > 0 && totalBonusPayments > 0) {\n        const compoundFactorSemi = Math.pow(1 + semiAnnualRate, totalBonusPayments);\n        baseBonusPayment = (bonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate * compoundFactorSemi) / (compoundFactorSemi - 1);\n      }\n    }\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(baseMonthlyPayment);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(baseMonthlyPayment);\n  } else {\n    // 元金均等返済\n    const monthlyPrincipalPerMonth = monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths;\n    const bonusPrincipalPerBonus = totalBonusPayments > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments : 0;\n\n    const firstMonthInterest = monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    const lastMonthInterest = monthlyPrincipalPerMonth * monthlyRate;\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalPerMonth + firstMonthInterest);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalPerMonth + lastMonthInterest);\n    baseMonthlyPayment = firstMonthPayment; // 代表値として初回を表示\n    baseBonusPayment = bonusPrincipalPerBonus;\n  }\n\n  // 2. 月次シミュレーションの実行（年次スケジュール構築）\n  let remMonthlyPrincipal = monthlyPrincipal;\n  let remBonusPrincipal = bonusPrincipal;\n\n  let cumulativePrincipal = 0;\n  let cumulativeInterest = 0;\n\n  const yearlySchedule: YearlySchedule[] = [];\n\n  let currentYearPrincipal = 0;\n  let currentYearInterest = 0;\n\n  for (let m = 1; m <= totalMonths; m++) {\n    const isBonusMonth = m % 6 === 0;\n\n    // 月々の元金・利息計算\n    let mInterest = 0;\n    let mPrincipal = 0;\n\n    if (repaymentType === 'equal-principal-and-interest') {\n      if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n        mPrincipal = Math.min(remMonthlyPrincipal, monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths);\n        mInterest = 0;\n      } else {\n        mInterest = remMonthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n        mPrincipal = Math.min(remMonthlyPrincipal, baseMonthlyPayment - mInterest);\n      }\n    } else {\n      // 元金均等\n      mPrincipal = Math.min(remMonthlyPrincipal, monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths);\n      mInterest = remMonthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    }\n\n    if (m === totalMonths) {\n      // 最終月の端数調整\n      mPrincipal = remMonthlyPrincipal;\n    }\n    remMonthlyPrincipal = Math.max(0, remMonthlyPrincipal - mPrincipal);\n\n    // ボーナス分の計算（6ヶ月ごと）\n    let bInterest = 0;\n    let bPrincipal = 0;\n\n    if (isBonusMonth && bonusPrincipal > 0) {\n      if (repaymentType === 'equal-principal-and-interest') {\n        if (semiAnnualRate === 0) {\n          bPrincipal = Math.min(remBonusPrincipal, bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments);\n          bInterest = 0;\n        } else {\n          bInterest = remBonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate;\n          bPrincipal = Math.min(remBonusPrincipal, baseBonusPayment - bInterest);\n        }\n      } else {\n        bPrincipal = Math.min(remBonusPrincipal, bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments);\n        bInterest = remBonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate;\n      }\n\n      if (m === totalMonths) {\n        // 最終回端数調整\n        bPrincipal = remBonusPrincipal;\n      }\n      remBonusPrincipal = Math.max(0, remBonusPrincipal - bPrincipal);\n    }\n\n    const monthTotalPrincipal = mPrincipal + bPrincipal;\n    const monthTotalInterest = mInterest + bInterest;\n\n    currentYearPrincipal += monthTotalPrincipal;\n    currentYearInterest += monthTotalInterest;\n    cumulativePrincipal += monthTotalPrincipal;\n    cumulativeInterest += monthTotalInterest;\n\n    // 年末または最終月の場合、年間集計を追加\n    if (m % 12 === 0 || m === totalMonths) {\n      const yearIndex = Math.ceil(m / 12);\n      const remainingBalance = Math.max(0, loanAmount - cumulativePrincipal);\n\n      yearlySchedule.push({\n        year: yearIndex,\n        annualPayment: Math.round(currentYearPrincipal + currentYearInterest),\n        principalPayment: Math.round(currentYearPrincipal),\n        interestPayment: Math.round(currentYearInterest),\n        remainingBalance: Math.round(remainingBalance),\n        cumulativeInterest: Math.round(cumulativeInterest),\n        cumulativePrincipal: Math.round(cumulativePrincipal)\n      });\n\n      currentYearPrincipal = 0;\n      currentYearInterest = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const roundedTotalInterest = Math.round(cumulativeInterest);\n  const roundedTotalPayment = Math.round(loanAmount + roundedTotalInterest);\n\n  return {\n    monthlyPayment: Math.round(baseMonthlyPayment),\n    firstMonthPayment,\n    lastMonthPayment,\n    totalPayment: roundedTotalPayment,\n    totalInterest: roundedTotalInterest,\n    yearlySchedule\n  };\n};\n\n/**\n * 金額を日本の「◯億◯◯◯万円」または「◯◯万円」にフォーマットする補助関数\n */\nexport const formatYenToKanji = (amount: number): string => {\n  if (amount <= 0) return '0円';\n\n  const oku = Math.floor(amount / 100000000);\n  const man = Math.floor((amount % 100000000) / 10000);\n  const remainder = Math.round(amount % 10000);\n\n  const parts: string[] = [];\n  if (oku > 0) parts.push(`${oku}億`);\n  if (man > 0) parts.push(`${man.toLocaleString()}万`);\n  if (remainder > 0 && oku === 0) parts.push(`${remainder.toLocaleString()}`);\n\n  return parts.length > 0 ? `${parts.join('')}円` : '0円';\n};","language":"typescript"}

const [bonusStr, setBonusStr] = useState(String(currentBonusRatio));\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setAmountStr(String(currentAmountInMan));\n  }, [currentAmountInMan]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setRateStr(String(currentRate));\n  }, [currentRate]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setTermStr(String(currentTerm));\n  }, [currentTerm]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n onChange?.({\n        ...values,\n        loanAmount: val * 10000\n      });\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleAmountBlur = () => {\n    const val = Number(amountStr);\n    const clamped = isNaN(val) || event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-toa","file":"src/utils/loanCalculator.ts","content":"import { LoanInput, LoanResult, YearlySchedule } from '../types';\n\n/**\n * 住宅ローン返済シミュレーション計算\n * - 元利均等返済 (equal-principal-and-interest)\n * - 元金均等返済 (equal-principal)\n * ボーナス併用（年2回加算）にも対応\n */\nexport const calculateLoan = (input: LoanInput): LoanResult => {\n  const {\n    loanAmount,\n    interestRate,\n    loanTermYears,\n    repaymentType,\n    bonusPaymentRatio\n  } = input;\n\n  if (loanAmount <= 0 || loanTermYears <= 0) {\n    return {\n      monthlyPayment: 0,\n      bonusPaymentAmount: 0,\n      firstMonthPayment: 0,\n      lastMonthPayment: 0,\n      totalPayment: 0,\n      totalInterest: 0,\n      yearlySchedule: []\n    };\n  }\n\n  const totalMonths = loanTermYears * 12;\n  const totalBonusPayments = loanTermYears * 2;\n  const monthlyRate = interestRate > 0 ? (interestRate / 100) / 12 : 0;\n  const semiAnnualRate = interestRate > 0 ? (interestRate / 100) / 2 : 0;\n\n  // ボーナス返済割合の正規化 (0〜40% を 0〜0.4 に変換)\n  const bonusRatio = Math.min(Math.max(bonusPaymentRatio, 0), 40) / 100;\n  const bonusPrincipal = Math.round(loanAmount * bonusRatio);\n  const monthlyPrincipal = loanAmount - bonusPrincipal;\n\n  // 1. 月々返済額とボーナス返済額の計算\n  let baseMonthlyPayment = 0;\n  let baseBonusPayment = 0;\n  let firstMonthPayment = 0;\n  let lastMonthPayment = 0;\n\n  if (repaymentType === 'equal-principal-and-interest') {\n    // 元利均等返済\n    if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n      baseMonthlyPayment = totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths : 0;\n    } else {\n      const compoundFactorMonthly = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, totalMonths);\n      baseMonthlyPayment = compoundFactorMonthly > 1\n        ? (monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate * compoundFactorMonthly) / (compoundFactorMonthly - 1)\n        : (totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths : 0);\n    }\n\n    if (bonusPrincipal > 0 && totalBonusPayments > 0) {\n      if (semiAnnualRate === 0) {\n        baseBonusPayment = bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments;\n      } else {\n        const compoundFactorSemi = Math.pow(1 + semiAnnualRate, totalBonusPayments);\n        baseBonusPayment = compoundFactorSemi > 1\n          ? (bonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate * compoundFactorSemi) / (compoundFactorSemi - 1)\n          : bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments;\n      }\n    } else {\n      baseBonusPayment = 0;\n    }\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(baseMonthlyPayment);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(baseMonthlyPayment);\n  } else {\n    // 元金均等返済\n    const monthlyPrincipalPerMonth = totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths : 0;\n    const bonusPrincipalPerBonus = totalBonusPayments > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments : 0;\n\n    const firstMonthInterest = monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    const lastMonthInterest = monthlyPrincipalPerMonth * monthlyRate;\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalPerMonth + firstMonthInterest);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalPerMonth + lastMonthInterest);\n    baseMonthlyPayment = firstMonthPayment; // 代表値として初回を表示\n\n    // 初回ボーナス加算額（元金充当額 ＋ 初回6ヶ月分利息）\n    if (bonusPrincipal > 0 && totalBonusPayments > 0) {\n      const firstBonusInterest = bonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate;\n      baseBonusPayment = bonusPrincipalPerBonus + firstBonusInterest;\n    } else {\n      baseBonusPayment = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 2. 月次シミュレーションの実行（年次スケジュール構築）\n  let remMonthlyPrincipal = monthlyPrincipal;\n  let remBonusPrincipal = bonusPrincipal;\n\n  let cumulativePrincipal = 0;\n  let cumulativeInterest = 0;\n\n  const yearlySchedule: YearlySchedule[] = [];\n\n  let currentYearPrincipal = 0;\n  let currentYearInterest = 0;\n\n  for (let m = 1; m <= totalMonths; m++) {\n    const isBonusMonth = m % 6 === 0;\n\n    // 月々の元金・利息計算\n    let mInterest = 0;\n    let mPrincipal = 0;\n\n    if (repaymentType === 'equal-principal-and-interest') {\n      if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n        mPrincipal = Math.min(remMonthlyPrincipal, monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths);\n        mInterest = 0;\n      } else {\n        mInterest = remMonthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n        mPrincipal = Math.min(remMonthlyPrincipal, baseMonthlyPayment - mInterest);\n      }\n    } else {\n      // 元金均等\n      mPrincipal = Math.min(remMonthlyPrincipal, monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths);\n      mInterest = remMonthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    }\n\n    if (m === totalMonths) {\n      // 最終月の端数調整\n      mPrincipal = remMonthlyPrincipal;\n    }\n    remMonthlyPrincipal = Math.max(0, remMonthlyPrincipal - mPrincipal);\n\n    // ボーナス分の計算（6ヶ月ごと）\n    let bInterest = 0;\n    let bPrincipal = 0;\n\n    if (isBonusMonth && bonusPrincipal > 0) {\n      if (repaymentType === 'equal-principal-and-interest') {\n        if (semiAnnualRate === 0) {\n          bPrincipal = Math.min(remBonusPrincipal, bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments);\n          bInterest = 0;\n        } else {\n          bInterest = remBonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate;\n          bPrincipal = Math.min(remBonusPrincipal, baseBonusPayment - bInterest);\n        }\n      } else {\n        bPrincipal = Math.min(remBonusPrincipal, bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments);\n        bInterest = remBonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate;\n      }\n\n      if (m === totalMonths) {\n        // 最終回端数調整\n        bPrincipal = remBonusPrincipal;\n      }\n      remBonusPrincipal = Math.max(0, remBonusPrincipal - bPrincipal);\n    }\n\n    const monthTotalPrincipal = mPrincipal + bPrincipal;\n    const monthTotalInterest = mInterest + bInterest;\n\n    currentYearPrincipal += monthTotalPrincipal;\n    currentYearInterest += monthTotalInterest;\n    cumulativePrincipal += monthTotalPrincipal;\n    cumulativeInterest += monthTotalInterest;\n\n    // 年末または最終月の場合、年間集計を追加\n    if (m % 12 === 0 || m === totalMonths) {\n      const yearIndex = Math.ceil(m / 12);\n      const remainingBalance = Math.max(0, loanAmount - cumulativePrincipal);\n\n      yearlySchedule.push({\n        year: yearIndex,\n        annualPayment: Math.round(currentYearPrincipal + currentYearInterest),\n        principalPayment: Math.round(currentYearPrincipal),\n        interestPayment: Math.round(currentYearInterest),\n        remainingBalance: Math.round(remainingBalance),\n        cumulativeInterest: Math.round(cumulativeInterest),\n        cumulativePrincipal: Math.round(cumulativePrincipal)\n      });\n\n      currentYearPrincipal = 0;\n      currentYearInterest = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const roundedTotalInterest = Math.round(cumulativeInterest);\n  const roundedTotalPayment = Math.round(loanAmount + roundedTotalInterest);\n\n  return {\n    monthlyPayment: Math.round(baseMonthlyPayment),\n    bonusPaymentAmount: Math.round(baseBonusPayment),\n    firstMonthPayment,\n    lastMonthPayment,\n    totalPayment: roundedTotalPayment,\n    totalInterest: roundedTotalInterest,\n    yearlySchedule\n  };\n};\n\n/**\n * 金額を日本の「◯億◯◯◯万円」または「◯◯万円」にフォーマットする補助関数\n * 万単位未満は四捨五入してすっきりと表示します。\n */\nexport const formatYenToKanji = (amount: number): string => {\n  if (amount <= 0) return '0円';\n\n  const totalMan = Math.round(amount / 10000);\n  if (totalMan === 0) {\n    return `${Math.round(amount).toLocaleString()}円`;\n  }\n\n  const oku = Math.floor(totalMan / 10000);\n  const man = totalMan % 10000;\n\n  if (oku > 0) {\n    if (man === 0) {\n      return `${oku}億円`;\n    }\n    return `${oku}億${man.toLocaleString()}万円`;\n  }\n\n  return `${man.toLocaleString()}万円`;\n};","language":"typescript"}

*/\n.recharts-default-tooltip {\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md) !important;\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border) !important;\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-md) !important;\n  font-family: number; // 単位: 円\n  interestRate: number; // 単位: % (例: 0.65)\n  loanTermYears: number; // 単位: 年 (例: 35)\n  repaymentType: RepaymentType;\n  bonusPaymentRatio: number; // 単位: % (0〜40)\n}\n\nexport interface YearlySchedule {\n  year: number;\n  annualPayment: number;\n  principalPayment: number;\n  interestPayment: number;\n  remainingBalance: number;\n  cumulativeInterest: number;\n  cumulativePrincipal: number;\n}\n\nexport interface LoanResult {\n  monthlyPayment: number;\n  bonusPaymentAmount: number; // ボーナス月加算額（年2回・1回あたり）\n  firstMonthPayment?: number;\n  lastMonthPayment?: number;\n  totalPayment: number;\n  totalInterest: number;\n  yearlySchedule: YearlySchedule[];\n}\n\nexport interface LoanFormProps {\n  values: LoanInput;\n  onChange?: (values: LoanInput) => YearlySchedule[];\n}","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/utils/loanCalculator.ts","content":"import { LoanInput, LoanResult, YearlySchedule } from '../types';\n\n/**\n * 住宅ローン返済シミュレーション計算\n * - 元利均等返済 (equal-principal-and-interest)\n * - 元金均等返済 (equal-principal)\n * ボーナス併用（年2回加算）にも対応\n */\nexport const calculateLoan = (input: LoanInput): LoanResult => {\n  const {\n    loanAmount,\n    interestRate,\n    loanTermYears,\n    repaymentType,\n    bonusPaymentRatio\n  } = input;\n\n  if (loanAmount <= 0 || loanTermYears <= 0) {\n    return {\n      monthlyPayment: 0,\n      bonusPaymentAmount: 0,\n      firstMonthPayment: 0,\n      lastMonthPayment: 0,\n      totalPayment: 0,\n      totalInterest: 0,\n      yearlySchedule: []\n    };\n  }\n\n  const totalMonths = loanTermYears * 12;\n  const totalBonusPayments = loanTermYears * 2;\n  const monthlyRate = interestRate > 0 ? (interestRate / 100) / 12 : 0;\n  const semiAnnualRate = interestRate > 0 ? (interestRate / 100) / 2 : 0;\n\n  // ボーナス返済割合の正規化 (0〜40% を 0〜0.4 に変換)\n  const bonusRatio = Math.min(Math.max(bonusPaymentRatio, 0), 40) / 100;\n  const bonusPrincipal = Math.round(loanAmount * bonusRatio);\n  const monthlyPrincipal = loanAmount - bonusPrincipal;\n\n  // 1. 月々返済額とボーナス返済額の計算\n  let baseMonthlyPayment = 0;\n  let baseBonusPayment = 0;\n  let firstMonthPayment = 0;\n  let lastMonthPayment = 0;\n\n  if (repaymentType === 'equal-principal-and-interest') {\n    // 元利均等返済\n    if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n      baseMonthlyPayment = totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths : 0;\n    } else {\n      const compoundFactorMonthly = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, totalMonths);\n      baseMonthlyPayment = compoundFactorMonthly > 1\n        ? (monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate * compoundFactorMonthly) / (compoundFactorMonthly - 1)\n        : (totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths : 0);\n    }\n\n    if (bonusPrincipal > 0 && totalBonusPayments > 0) {\n      if (semiAnnualRate === 0) {\n        baseBonusPayment = bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments;\n      } else {\n        const compoundFactorSemi = Math.pow(1 + semiAnnualRate, totalBonusPayments);\n        baseBonusPayment = compoundFactorSemi > 1\n          ? (bonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate * compoundFactorSemi) / (compoundFactorSemi - 1)\n          : bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments;\n      }\n    } else {\n      baseBonusPayment = 0;\n    }\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(baseMonthlyPayment);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(baseMonthlyPayment);\n  } else {\n    // 元金均等返済\n    const monthlyPrincipalPerMonth = totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths : 0;\n    const bonusPrincipalPerBonus = totalBonusPayments > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments : 0;\n\n    const firstMonthInterest = monthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    const lastMonthInterest = monthlyPrincipalPerMonth * monthlyRate;\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalPerMonth + firstMonthInterest);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalPerMonth + lastMonthInterest);\n    baseMonthlyPayment = firstMonthPayment; // 代表値として初回を表示\n\n    // 初回ボーナス加算額（元金充当額 ＋ 初回6ヶ月分利息）\n    if (bonusPrincipal > 0 && totalBonusPayments > 0) {\n      const firstBonusInterest = bonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate;\n      baseBonusPayment = bonusPrincipalPerBonus + firstBonusInterest;\n    } else {\n      baseBonusPayment = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 2. 月次シミュレーションの実行（年次スケジュール構築）\n  let remMonthlyPrincipal = monthlyPrincipal;\n  let remBonusPrincipal = bonusPrincipal;\n\n  let cumulativePrincipal = 0;\n  let cumulativeInterest = 0;\n\n  const yearlySchedule: YearlySchedule[] = [];\n\n  let currentYearPrincipal = 0;\n  let currentYearInterest = 0;\n\n  for (let m = 1; m <= totalMonths; m++) {\n    const isBonusMonth = m % 6 === 0;\n\n    // 月々の元金・利息計算\n    let mInterest = 0;\n    let mPrincipal = 0;\n\n    if (repaymentType === 'equal-principal-and-interest') {\n      if (monthlyRate === 0) {\n        mPrincipal = Math.min(remMonthlyPrincipal, monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths);\n        mInterest = 0;\n      } else {\n        mInterest = remMonthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n        mPrincipal = Math.min(remMonthlyPrincipal, baseMonthlyPayment - mInterest);\n      }\n    } else {\n      // 元金均等\n      mPrincipal = Math.min(remMonthlyPrincipal, monthlyPrincipal / totalMonths);\n      mInterest = remMonthlyPrincipal * monthlyRate;\n    }\n\n    if (m === totalMonths) {\n      // 最終月の端数調整\n      mPrincipal = remMonthlyPrincipal;\n    }\n    remMonthlyPrincipal = Math.max(0, remMonthlyPrincipal - mPrincipal);\n\n    // ボーナス分の計算（6ヶ月ごと）\n    let bInterest = 0;\n    let bPrincipal = 0;\n\n    if (isBonusMonth && bonusPrincipal > 0) {\n      if (repaymentType === 'equal-principal-and-interest') {\n        if (semiAnnualRate === 0) {\n          bPrincipal = Math.min(remBonusPrincipal, bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments);\n          bInterest = 0;\n        } else {\n          bInterest = remBonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate;\n          bPrincipal = Math.min(remBonusPrincipal, baseBonusPayment - bInterest);\n        }\n      } else {\n        bPrincipal = Math.min(remBonusPrincipal, bonusPrincipal / totalBonusPayments);\n        bInterest = remBonusPrincipal * semiAnnualRate;\n      }\n\n      if (m === totalMonths) {\n        // 最終回端数調整\n        bPrincipal = remBonusPrincipal;\n      }\n      remBonusPrincipal = Math.max(0, remBonusPrincipal - bPrincipal);\n    }\n\n    const monthTotalPrincipal = mPrincipal + bPrincipal;\n    const monthTotalInterest = mInterest + bInterest;\n\n    currentYearPrincipal += monthTotalPrincipal;\n    currentYearInterest += monthTotalInterest;\n    cumulativePrincipal += monthTotalPrincipal;\n    cumulativeInterest += monthTotalInterest;\n\n    // 年末または最終月の場合、年間集計を追加\n    if (m % 12 === 0 || m === totalMonths) {\n      const yearIndex = Math.ceil(m / 12);\n      const remainingBalance = Math.max(0, loanAmount - cumulativePrincipal);\n\n      yearlySchedule.push({\n        year: yearIndex,\n        annualPayment: Math.round(currentYearPrincipal + currentYearInterest),\n        principalPayment: Math.round(currentYearPrincipal),\n        interestPayment: Math.round(currentYearInterest),\n        remainingBalance: Math.round(remainingBalance),\n        cumulativeInterest: Math.round(cumulativeInterest),\n        cumulativePrincipal: Math.round(cumulativePrincipal)\n      });\n\n      currentYearPrincipal = 0;\n      currentYearInterest = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const roundedTotalInterest = Math.round(cumulativeInterest);\n  const roundedTotalPayment = Math.round(loanAmount + roundedTotalInterest);\n\n  return {\n    monthlyPayment: Math.round(baseMonthlyPayment),\n    bonusPaymentAmount: Math.round(baseBonusPayment),\n    firstMonthPayment,\n    lastMonthPayment,\n    totalPayment: roundedTotalPayment,\n    totalInterest: roundedTotalInterest,\n    yearlySchedule\n  };\n};\n\n/**\n * 金額を日本の「◯億◯◯◯万円」または「◯◯万円」にフォーマットする補助関数\n * 万単位未満は四捨五入してすっきりと表示します。\n */\nexport const formatYenToKanji = (amount: number): string => {\n  if (amount <= 0) return '0円';\n\n  const totalMan = Math.round(amount / 10000);\n  if (totalMan === 0) {\n    return `${Math.round(amount).toLocaleString()}円`;\n  }\n\n  const oku = Math.floor(totalMan / 10000);\n  const man = totalMan % 10000;\n\n  if (oku > 0) {\n    if (man === 0) {\n      return `${oku}億円`;\n    }\n    return `${oku}億${man.toLocaleString()}万円`;\n  }\n\n  return `${man.toLocaleString()}万円`;\n};","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/components/LoanForm.tsx","content":"import { useState, useEffect, ChangeEvent } from 'react';\nimport { LoanFormProps, setTermStr(String(currentTerm));\n  }, [currentTerm]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setBonusStr(String(currentBonusRatio));\n  }, [currentBonusRatio]);\n\n  // スライダー変更ハンドラ\n  const ({bonusAmountInMan.toLocaleString()}万円分)\n            </span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <input\n          id=\"bonus-ratio-slider\"\n          type=\"range\"\n          min={0}\n
